# SPEC-0058: Main story progression

- Answers: proactive, none requested (main story order: quest table story rows, acts and heroes, walkthrough from New Game to the endings); R-0099, R-0103 (flag 239's live setter path); R-0104 (Rope Gun source and Glade branch; physical traversal need remains open); R-0148 (Gil the Fence, Drool and Lump in pack 1139 are talkers, not fights); R-0151 (ordered goal list for an automated player, Act 1); proactive (verify pass: pack 1397 plate scenes and lift order, start-room join); R-0197 (goal 374: the burrow gates); R-0215 (the timeout ending's tick numbers and what follows movie 80); R-0218 (goal 1: flag 706 is set again in the opening, by the Farm activation thread); R-0219 (goals 28, 300, 301: flag 249 stays clear); R-0220 (goals 70, 72, 88: hall volumes, floors and stand points); R-0222 (goals 113 and 114: Painter Howell is present straight after the council scene); R-0223 (goal 556: how stage 4 of the Petpet shrine is won); R-0225 (the shop copies beside 1427)
- Status: Draft
- Disc build: SCUS-97367 v1.00 (same fingerprint as SPEC-0001)
- Confidence: per section. Quest row kind, title and objective text fields and the area-name lookup: High. Act-start calls, act flags and swap modes: High. Stage-table checkpoint meaning: Medium-High. Order of steps inside an act: Medium (quest-row order plus flag dependencies). Boss names per pack: Medium; boss instances 1, 5 and 12: High for the script match, Medium-High for the entity order. Routes between areas: Medium-Low where they rest on cell exits or the area-pair table. Return-world calls (1174 to 1300, 1327 to 886, 1122 to 1300, 1428 to 1411, 1411 to 1432, 1432 to 1300): High. Pack 1432 has no entry other than the 1411 return world: High. Script setters of the main-story flags: High for the setter sites, Medium for what each puzzle is. Pack reach (SPEC-0006): 174 of 191 packs are enterable from New Game. Per-step confidence is in the walkthrough table.

## Summary

The main story is a chain of progress flags (SPEC-0049) that level scripts (SPEC-0030 and the level-script specs SPEC-0032, SPEC-0037, SPEC-0039, SPEC-0041 to SPEC-0045) set and test across four acts. The game's own quest table names every step: its first 158 rows are the main story and its last 80 rows are side quests. A step is listed in the journal while its start flag is on and its done flag is off (SPEC-0049, Quest steps and the journal). The player controls Tor alone in Act 1, Roberta alone in Act 2, and both heroes with free switching from the start of Act 3 (SPEC-0053). Each act begins with an act-start call. The story ends in the Darkest Faerie fight (SPEC-0054), either with the finale movie, which leads through the epilogue in pack 1300 to the credits, or with the timeout ending. With this spec the clean side can lay out the story order, check that every story step has a flag setter, a route into its area and a way out, and build an automated story test that walks the steps in order.

## Where it lives on the disc

- Quest rows: archive resource 533, data table 1 (238 rows of 36 bytes; SPEC-0024 table 1 and SPEC-0049 Quest rows). Rows 0 to 157 are the main story, rows 158 to 237 side quests. High.
- Area names: archive resource 533, data table 5 (77 rows of 8 bytes; SPEC-0024 table 5). The quest row's scene id indexes this table. High.
- Stage table (the start-point checkpoints): archive resource 533, table 11 (SPEC-0049, Stage table rows).
- Act starts, flag writes and tests, movies, boss-fight instances, item gifts and area changes: the level scripts of the world packs named below (SPEC-0030 calls TriggerActStart, SetGameState, QueryGameState, PlayFMV, BossFightTweakHero, GiveItemToHero, SetHeroSwapMode, SwitchHero, SetGameOver and the transition calls). Packs are archive resource numbers (SPEC-0006).
- Area-pair table: boot program data at virtual address 0x31EEE0, 25 entries of two 32-bit values (SPEC-0006, area-pair test). The values are world-pack archive resource numbers: 23 pairs, then two zero entries. Read it at run time; it is not copied here. Medium-High for the values being pack numbers.

## Layout

### Quest row fields used by the story (table 1)

This adds meanings to the SPEC-0049 layout; the other fields are as given there.

| Offset | Size | Type | Meaning | Confidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| +0x00 | 4 | u32 | Row kind: 0 = main story, 1 = side quest. Rows 0 to 157 hold 0, rows 158 to 237 hold 1. | High |
| +0x04 | 4 | s32 | Start flag (SPEC-0049). | High |
| +0x08 | 4 | s32 | Done flag (SPEC-0049). | High |
| +0x0C | 4 | u32 | Title text: offset from the start of the table block (SPEC-0024, Pointers and string relocation) to a NUL-terminated single-byte string. | High |
| +0x10 | 4 | u32 | Objective text: same rule as +0x0C. All 238 resolve. | High |
| +0x20 | 4 | s32 | Scene id: row index into table 5 (area names), or -1 for no single area. | High for the lookup |

### Area names (table 5)

| Offset | Size | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| +0x00 | 4 | u32 | Area name: offset from the start of the table block to a NUL-terminated string. All 77 resolve. |
| +0x04 | 4 | u32 | Run-time count (SPEC-0024); zero on disc. |

The area names are game text; they are read from the disc at run time and are not copied here.

### Act structure by quest rows

| Act | Main quest rows | Hero control |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 0 to 69 | Tor only |
| 2 | 70 to 89 | Roberta only |
| 3 | 90 to 115 and 121 to 141 | Both |
| 4 | 116 to 120 and 142 to 157 | Both |

Rows 116 to 120 sit between Act 3 rows in table order but belong to Act 4 (the rescue of Queen Fyora and the Orb). Medium-High.

## Behavior

### Acts and heroes

| Act | Starts in pack | Act-start stage (SPEC-0053 3.7) | Swap mode after | Progress flag stored | Intro movie |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 909 | 1 | 0 (locked to Tor) | none | 88 (with movie 1) |
| 2 | 1174 | 2 | 1 (locked to Roberta) | 220 | 89 |
| 3 | 1327 | 3 | 2 (free; Tor selected) | 248 | 90 |
| 4 | 1428 | 4 | 2 (free) | 332 | 91 |

Confidence: High for the calls and stored flags. The heroes join for good at the Act 3 start, at the end of Roberta's escape from Faerieland. In pack 909 the act-start call is made after movies 88 and 1 are queued and before they are shown: queued movies play at that pass's movie step, in queue order, so the act start is already applied when movie 88 is shown (High; step 2).

Local hero locks inside Acts 3 and 4 (Medium): pack 1244 sets mode 1 or 0 and activates one hero; pack 1246 sets mode 2 after the Werelupe King; packs 1371 and 1394 switch heroes and set mode 3 during their puzzles; pack 1432 switches heroes during the final fight (SPEC-0054). In packs 1122, 1217 and 1428, a dead Roberta sends the game to game over when Tor is active, or locks control to Roberta when she is active, unless a Potion of Revival (item 50) is held (SPEC-0055, SPEC-0026).

### Stage table as checkpoints

The start-point chooser labels (SPEC-0035) match the stage ids of the stage table one to one. Each stage is a designer checkpoint of the flag state:

| Stage id | Checkpoint |
| --- | --- |
| 0 | Act 1 done |
| 1 | Act 2 done |
| 2 | Act 3 done |
| 3 | Act 3 middle |
| 4 | Squire accepted |
| 5 | Squire chores done |
| 6 | Act 3 up to entering the dungeons |
| 7 | Act 4 legends done |

Confidence: Medium-High (from the chooser labels and the flags each stage sets). The chooser always loads the default packs (SPEC-0006), so a stage start sets flags but does not move the hero. The stage contents are a useful cross-check for the walkthrough below.

"OQ n" points to an open question that leaves a link in the chain unexplained.

### Endings

Confidence: High for the calls, the epilogue setters and the credits gate; Medium that the hero reaches the epilogue trigger volumes.

1. Finale (pack 1432, level flag 4; SPEC-0054 and SPEC-0049 Ending; the fight is entered at locator 0 in the alcove, see the final-fight paragraph below): movie 85 plays, then the game returns to pack 1300 (world 151, entry message value 80) with progress flags 94 and 365 on.
2. Epilogue: the finale's return adds blackboard message 2 with value 80 for world 151 (pack 1300). Pack 1300's activate-world callback reads that value, sets flag 366 and places the hero at landing 2 (Open question 9). Script trigger 2 (366 on, 367 off) sets 367 and plays movie 79. Script trigger 3 (367 on, 368 off) fades out, plays movie 83 and sets 368. The finale runs when the hero reaches the trigger-1 cylinder of pack 1432 inside the 15 s landed window (SPEC-0054, Trigger 1 and the finale gate). High (no play check).
3. Credits: on the next play pass after 368 is set, the in-play gate on flag 368 sets the credits-pending flag and opens the credits screen (SPEC-0026, Credits; SPEC-0035, section 6). Cross and Triangle are ignored during this roll. At its end the world is torn down, which clears every flag, and the front end returns to its boot loop (logo, attract, title). The credits can also be opened from the front-end Options page; that roll returns to Options.
4. Timeout ending: when the final fight's clock reaches 0, the screen fades out (0.5 s), movie 80 is queued and the game-over flag is set (SetGameOver(1)) on the same tick, and movie 80 is shown in that pass. The game-over page follows on the next pass; it is the same page a hero death leads to, and it freezes the world and waits for a new Cross press on page pass 122 or later. Nothing else is scripted after movie 80: no further movie, no credits and no return to another pack. Without a save or load made earlier in the session the exit goes to the card state, the logo, the attract screen and the title; with one, Continue re-applies that snapshot (SPEC-0026 and SPEC-0055). Test vector (Tor selected, no swaps, crossing the entry rectangle on tick 0): clock start on tick 74, flag 2 on tick 10,874, game over and movie 80 on tick 10,906, earliest exit about tick 11,029 (SPEC-0054, Test vectors). A hero game over is the right end state; it is not a death and changes no health.

### What the walkthrough shows about the chain

- **Rope Gun targets in Illusen's Glade (R-0104; High for the target data, Low for route necessity).** Pack 956 has three active aim targets at (-712.090, 79.337, -831.990), (-673.268, 53.885, -827.594) and (-720.682, 34.656, -838.226). The first lies 11.337 units above a y=68 surface; the third lies 33.344 units below that same surface. The middle target is 6.523 horizontal units from the nearest triangle in its closest walk mesh, whose height is y=8.312, 45.574 units below the target. The target points therefore do not form an obvious sequence of walkable landing points. Pack 956's item-36 branch activates the targets and changes Illusen's message, but it does not set Glade completion when item 36 is absent. This geometry and callback behavior do not establish that the Rope Gun is physically required to progress; navigation meshes omit jump arcs, ledge climbs and the complete collision route. Related pack variants repeat target lists, so a target's presence alone does not establish a unique story gate.
- The final-fight pack 1432 is entered only by the return world that movie 72's call sets in pack 1411. No cell exit and no other script call leads into it. That call needs flag 338, which only movie 71's call sets, and movie 71 needs flag 337. So flag 337 gates the final fight itself, not only the two movies. On arrival the hero is placed at locator 0 in the alcove (SPEC-0039, module 1432), followed by the fight-start step (SPEC-0054). Flag 337 is set by the six-pedestal puzzle in pack 1382, reached from 1411 through 1391 and 1381 (step 46; Open question 1).
- Reach (SPEC-0006): 174 of the 191 world packs are enterable from New Game. The main-path packs 745, 752, 777, 1017 and 1394 are among them (door, script load and cell routes in steps 32, 34, 30, 35 and 45). Two of them leave by script loads: 1017 back to 991 and 1394 back to 1353. Pack 1077 is an unused copy of pack 1129, whose module it shares; step 41 runs in 1139 and 1129. It stays one of the 17 unreached packs (High). The other unreached packs are duplicates (678, 686, 970), a second zone variant (1323), cut variants (1259, 1298), a cut pack (1170), a closed pair (683 and 1003), and seven packs that nothing names (684, 720, 742, 879, 1004, 1055, 1082).

## Goal list for an automated player (Act 1)

Purpose: an ordered list of concrete goals that takes a test player from New Game through the Gnarfas fight, built from the walkthrough above, the level-script specs (SPEC-0037, SPEC-0039, SPEC-0041 to SPEC-0045), SPEC-0049 (flags), SPEC-0022 (dialogue), SPEC-0021 (items), SPEC-0048 (interactables) and SPEC-0006 (doors and entries). Goals 1 to 99 cover steps 1 to 19 and the first scene of step 20. Confidence is per row (H High, M Medium, L Low); a row marked M or L names the link that is not settled.

How to read the table.
- Kinds: TALK (stand in the character's talk range and press Square; the entity id is the entity-table id of the pack), PICK (touch a world item), SQUARE (press Square inside a script-trigger volume or at a prompt), DOOR (enter a door rectangle and confirm the Enter prompt with Square), WALK (reach a point or volume), PUSH, CLIMB, FIGHT, GAME (a minigame with its win rule) and WAIT (a scene or movie runs; control returns at its end). A row with two kinds is done in the order named.
- Positions are world coordinates (x, y, z) of the pack named. A "slab" is a trigger rectangle given as footprint centre, width along its long axis, depth, and bottom y. A "cylinder" gives base centre, radius and height; a "sphere" gives centre and radius. A door rectangle is entered anywhere along its width; its centre is given. Floor y values are approximate (plus or minus 1).
- "Done" lists the flags (SPEC-0049) or items that are true when the goal has been met. After a load, skip a goal when its Done condition already holds. "Needs" lists flags or items that must be on first; a goal whose Needs is not met cannot succeed.
- Walking legs between packs need no goal of their own: the hero walks over the ground and the next pack streams in (SPEC-0006, cell exits and world swap). The ground neighbours used by Act 1 are: 909 - 691 - 1066 - 696; 696 - 1023 (town), 696 - 1184 (Shadowglen Woods), 696 - 1230, 696 - 1056 and 696 - 893; 893 - 851; 851 - 842 and 851 - 800; 1230 - 706 - 956. Pack 1184 leads into 1217 by a script trigger. The coordinates in a WALK row are the aim point inside the destination pack. High for the neighbour pairs (they come from the pack neighbour lists); the exact crossing point inside each pair is Medium.
- Interior exits: to leave an interior, walk into its exit rectangle (centre): 951 (17.5, -0.8, 22.5); 1148 (-0.1, -2.7, 16.2); 1150 (6.5, 0.0, 8.3); 1131 (0.0, 0.0, 17.5); 1133 (0.0, 0.0, 16.1). Each leads back to the exterior it was entered from (1023 for 1131, 1133, 1148 and 1150; the farm for 951). No flag is needed except where a row says so.
- Town doors of pack 1023 (door index, interior, centre): 0 pack 1098 (65.5, 23.9, 609.5); 1 pack 1150 (-126.0, 24.4, 482.1); 2 pack 1172 (349.9, 26.5, 383.4); 3 pack 1143 (383.1, 24.3, 532.8); 4 pack 1136 (386.5, 42.0, 854.8); 5 pack 1148 (193.4, 29.9, 866.1); 6 pack 1131 (7.3, 24.0, 817.1); 7 pack 1133 (-132.9, 26.3, 739.4); 8 pack 1140 (-28.0, 24.7, 390.8); 9 pack 1139 (433.8, 33.6, 649.3); 10 pack 1129 (404.8, 25.5, 659.2). Doors 1 to 8 need flag 136 on. Door 0 needs (150 on and 11 off) or (150 off and 15 on). Door 9 needs 13 on; door 10 needs 35 on (SPEC-0045, pack 1023).
- Entity ids for the farm family (pack 909): Lucy is entity 31 (ids 31 to 36 are her six regional placements), Patricia (Mum) entity 37 (ids 37 to 42) and Hubert (Dad) entity 43 (ids 43 to 50). The stage rule of SPEC-0045 (Activation thread) decides which placement is loaded, so talk to whichever of the family is standing in the farm.

Gaps settled in this list.
- Entity ids of the farm family (Lucy 31 to 36, Patricia 37 to 42, Hubert 43 to 50) come from the proximity routines' entity lists; step 3 of the walkthrough did not name them.
- Item sources for step 7: the shield (item 106) lies in pack 1131; items 40, 41 and 43 lie in the village; item 42 comes from the shop talker in 1148 (the sword trade gives it with no money); item 60 comes from the smith in 1133; item 38 from Torak. Item 1 (Letter of Consent) is never tested, so it is not needed. Item 0 comes from pack 951's story thread when 147 is on.
- The ground neighbour chain for the cell routes, and the aim point in each pack (positions from the pack trigger and locator tables).
- Pack 1171's route: it is entered from 1173 (not from 1098 directly), through 1152; the lever in 1173 opens the way only after flag 162 is on (set by the 1152 villager).
- Door and trigger volumes for every goal that needs a position, taken from the packs' trigger tables and matched to the module specs where they give one (trigger indices, extents and the footprint centre rule of SPEC-0028).
- The farm pliers route is a crate push plus two ladders (SPEC-0048); Medium.
- The 1148 talkers (Kendrick, the shop talker) and the route from the lift top in 956 to the 985 entry slab (goal 96), both listed in the rows above.

Open items and defaults.
- Pliers route (goals 11 to 13): Resolved (M): the shelf, the loft floor at y 61.4, the second ladder and the roof are connected as goal 12 describes; the ladder grab from the loft is L. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): follow goals 10 to 12 and report the goal blocked when the item is not reached.
- Resolved: the positions of entities 35, 36, 39 and 49 in 1098 (goals 51, 70, 72, 77, 88), the breakables of 1171 and 1056 (goals 64 and 71) and the talkers of 1133, 1150, 1152 and 1163 come from the entity-id table and the character regions it names. Resolved (goals 32 and 36): the pack's own character block holds three characters in 1148 (ordinals 0 to 2: Guardsman Yavik at (34.6, 0.0, -20.0), Officer Kendrick at (-0.1, 0.0, -34.9) and Tark Gutterunner at (26.0, 1.9, -27.2)); the entity table sends entity 1 to Kendrick and entity 3 to Tark, and all three stand still. H for the names and points (the block's placement records are read in the same layout as the character regions). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): talk to the nearest talker.
- Resolved (goal 94): the platform volume runs the ride and the two spheres only call it. Resolved (goal 96): the walk to the 985 entry slab is the long climbing walk listed in the goal row (about 1,120 units, waypoints with heights); M for the line, L for a few single height jumps of up to about 2.9 along it.
- Resolved (goal 41): a world swap back into the farm re-runs its activation and plays movie 5 (H for the rule). Fallback if the movie does not play: leave 909 through house door 0 and come out again.
- Attack-state numbers in goals 54, 59 and 68 refer to the attack table of SPEC-0036.

## Goal list for an automated player (Act 2)

Purpose: the goals that carry a test player from the aftermath in pack 956 (goal 99) through the end of Act 2 and the Act 3 start, built from walkthrough steps 21 to 27, the module sections of SPEC-0043, SPEC-0044, SPEC-0045, SPEC-0037, SPEC-0039 and SPEC-0041, SPEC-0048 (blocks), SPEC-0028 (the pad puzzle of pack 1299), SPEC-0053 (hero setup) and SPEC-0021 (items). Goals 100 to 155. Conventions, kinds, position rules and confidence tags are those of the Act 1 list. Positions are in the pack named; a slab is given as footprint centre, width, depth and bottom y, a plane as centre, width and bottom y.

Heroes, items and bosses in Act 2.
- Goals 100 to 110 are played by Tor (swap mode 0). Goal 111 (the Act 2 start) makes Roberta the selected hero and locks the mode to Roberta (mode 1) until goal 155. No hero switch is possible or needed in between. At goal 155 (Act 3 start) the mode becomes free and Tor is selected (SPEC-0053, section 3.7). High.
- Inventory: at goal 111 the whole Act 1 inventory and wallet go to a stash and Roberta receives Red Juppies x3 (150), Purple Juppies x3 (149), the Diplomat Dress (127) and 1,150 Neopoints. So Tor's Light Mote (170), Fire Mote (160) and sword are unavailable until goal 155 returns the stash. Roberta has no weapon until goal 121 and her attack button does nothing before then (SPEC-0036). Heal with a Juppie from the quick panel when health is low; there is no Potion of Revival in the kit, so a death in Act 2 is game over (SPEC-0055). High.
- Motes: the only Mote Act 2 needs is the Fire Mote (item 160) x100 that goal 125 gives. Equipping is optional: open the second quick panel with Down, pick the Fire Mote and confirm (SPEC-0021, Second quick panel and Mote equipping). Free Motes in pack 1300 (item 170 at (91.8, 1538.4, -78.8) and (-63.1, 1531.0, -105.3), item 162 groups, item 166, item 172) are optional pickups. Medium.
- Boss fights: none in Act 2 is won by killing. Boss instance 3 in pack 1174 (goals 104 to 110) is an escape run: the Faerie Sisters (alternate loop) cannot be hurt and the Darkest Faerie (loop A) stands still at (0, -126.25, 97.2) in section 0, throwing the hero when he comes within 10 horizontal units (no damage; SPEC-0011, SPEC-0054). Entry: goal 103 (hero placed at locator 0). Exit: goal 111 (end rectangle, trigger 1). The Faerie Sisters' real fight is Act 3 (step 43). High.

Walk from the entry: north along x about -7 to -10 to (-10, 1514, -35), up the slope to (-16, 1530, -92), (-7, 1536, -107), straight on to (-7, 1536, -122), then west and up round the hill: (-19, 1536, -137), (-34, 1537, -152), (-43, 1542, -149), (-46, 1547, -134), (-46, 1551, -119), (-46, 1553, -89), (-52, 1553, -83) (waypoints as (x, height, z); about 395 units). Gaps settled in this list.
- Where the escape run starts and which volume starts it: 1098's trigger 6 is on the second floor; the plane and rectangle triggers of 1174 with their planes; the section scenes and locators.
- Wand-part sources: item 10 and 11 are floor placements in pack 1300; item 62 is made from item 61 (Painter Howell) at trigger 0; Seradar removes only items 10 and 11.
- The order of the 1324 puzzle (stage 2 first), the pad touch sequence of 1299 and the exit door.
- The 1300, 1335, 1344, 1324 and 1327 teleport and trigger volumes.
- Positions of the talkers and the target (Howell, Seradar, the Shooting Spellbook) from the character regions named by the entity tables, and the walking routes of goals 102, 104, 114, 117, 119, 135 and 138, built from the pack ground, the climb panels and the stair, ramp and spiral surfaces; whether goals 131 and 132 can be skipped.

Open items and defaults.
- Resolved (goal 104): the 18 floor tiles (positions, short and long crumbles), the barrier lines with their damage boxes and the lanes that avoid them are listed in the goal row; the floor is continuous across the whole run. M for the lanes, L for whether a crumbled pad leaves a hole. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): follow the walk in the row; keep more than 4 units from tile pads.
- Resolved (goal 117): item 11 lies on a branch that is reached only by climbing the tree's wall panels (three tiers, ground to 1564.2); the grab fires from the ground with the stick pushed into the panel. M for the step from the panels onto the branch. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): as in the row; report blocked after 90 s.
- Resolved (goals 135 and 138): the walk from the 1324 entry down to the lower floor and up to the bell platform is a descending spiral then a ramp (waypoints in the rows, about 610 units from the entry to the bells). M.
- Resolved (goals 114, 119, 120 and 122): Painter Howell (entity 18), Seradar (entity 43) and the target (entity 0) are not in embedded blocks; the entity tables name character regions 1315, 1333 and 1328, and the positions are in the rows. H for the points, M for the walks.
- Resolved (goal 102): the stairs to the 1098 second floor are two mirror flights with a switchback and a gallery (waypoints in the row, about 200 units). M.
- Resolved (goals 131 and 132): flags 402 and 403 are read by nothing outside those two triggers, so both goals may be skipped (H).

## Goal list for an automated player (Act 3)

Purpose: the ordered goals for walkthrough steps 28 to 43, from the arrival in Brightvale (return world 64 after movie 30) to the end of Act 3 (movie 54 and the return to pack 1300; step 44 and Act 4 are not in this list). Same table form, kinds, positions and confidence tags as the Act 1 list (read its "How to read the table" first). Goals are numbered from 300 so that they do not clash with Act 1 (1 to 99) and Act 2 (100 on). Confidence is per row (H High, M Medium, L Low); a row marked M or L names the link that is not settled.

Act 3 conventions.
- Heroes. Act 3 starts with swap mode 2 (free) and Tor selected (SPEC-0053, section 3.7). Switch with the L1 and R1 chord. "Hero" in a Needs column names the hero that must be active; "either" means no restriction. The only forced lock in this range is pack 1244 (step 38), whose last scene sets mode 0 (Tor only) until the Werelupe King is dead; its death scene sets mode 2 again (goals 386 to 391).
- Walking legs. As in Act 1, a leg between packs needs no goal of its own beyond the aim point given. The ground neighbour chains used here (pack: neighbours) are: 886: 704, 699, 717; 704: 886, 880; 880: 704, 699, 702; 702: 880, 753, 1238; 1238: 702, 713; 713: 1238, 722, 735; 722: 713 (its five door interiors hang off it); 735: 713; 699: 886, 1230; 991: 699, 705; 705: 991, 986, 989; 696: 1023, 1056, 1230, 893, 1184, 1066; 893: 696, 851; 851: 893, 822, 842, 800; 822: 851, 861; 861: 822, 864; 864: 861, 874; 1250: 1255, 1256, 1254, 1251, 717; 1256: 1250, 1257; 1255: 1250, 1244, 1245, 1246; 1129: 1130; 1130: 1129, 1080; 1080: 1130, 685, 1079; 685: 1080, 1078; 1079: 1080, 1081; 1096 and 1081 are joined by a script load, not by cells. The crusher room 1089 and the hazard rooms 1090 and 1091 are side rooms of the Knights Crypts and are not on the main route (goal 452). High for the chains (they come from the pack neighbour lists).
- Script teleports. A "teleport" door is a rectangle of the pack (centre given, width along the long axis); stand inside it and press Square at the prompt (context 13) unless the row says it acts on contact. Pack 880 teleport 0, pack 991 teleports 4, 5 and 7, pack 753 teleport 0, pack 758 teleports 0 and 1, pack 777 teleport 0, pack 722 teleports 0 to 4, pack 1257 teleports 0 to 2, pack 1173 teleport 0 and pack 1152 teleports 0 and 1 are of this kind (SPEC-0006).
- Hero index in level scripts: hero 0 is Tor and hero 1 is Roberta.
- Boss arrivals and exits are given in SPEC-0062 (arrival records); the exit of every boss row below is the scripted one named in the row.

The other index-0 records of the pack are solid geometry, not prompts. The keep is a vertical climb: from the arena floor (y 102 to 126) ladders lead up to a west ledge (ladder tops at y 144.5 and 146, locators 4 to 7 at y 123 to 155), then climb panels at (951.8, 157.8, 236.7) and (971.3, 180.2, 236.9) lead to locator 8 (977.7, 185.5, 246.7), a tall panel run reaches locator 9 (977.7, 261.8, 246.7), and panels at (1062.7, 241.9, 208.6) and (1037.6, 257.1, 188.7) lead to the top. Resolved (the climb above y 145, with walls; M-L as a whole, the parts are marked). (a) The ladder tops at y 144.5 and 146 near (899.9, 146.0, -21.5) are a trap, not the way: the platform there (y 143.4, x 901 to 919, z -21 to -4) is a breakable floor, and script triggers 2 and 3 (rectangles at (908.8, 142.4, -17.8), 7.0 wide, and (906.7, 140.0, -23.3), 16.2 wide) break it and throw the hero by a long fall to locator 11 (911.8, 104.4, -11.2). Stay off them and use the south-west ladder. H. (b) Arena leg (x, y, z): (1094.7, 101.7, -1.5) arrival; (1002.0, 101.7, 86.0); (933.5, 101.7, 86.0), through the gap in the dividing wall at z 76 to 104 (x 946 to 962); then to the foot of ladder g14, (888.5, 101.7, 131.0), and up it (34.6 tall, top (885.6, 136.1, 132.0)). The wall records place a solid slab at z 112 to 117 (x 862 to 921, top 125 to 130) between the gap and the foot, and the wall-aware search left the last 20 units open, so this leg is L; the ground-only model reaches the foot directly. (c) West ledge (M): (884.7, 135.2, 131.0); (882.2, 141.7, 139.7); (883.5, 137.1, 174.7); (892.2, 139.9, 196.0); (887.2, 142.4, 218.5); (907.2, 147.6, 238.5); (949.7, 142.0, 239.7); climb panel up at (951.8, 157.8, 236.7) to (953.5, 157.7, 241.0); (969.7, 157.7, 241.0); climb panel up at (971.3, 180.2, 236.9) to (973.5, 180.0, 239.7); locator 8 (977.7, 185.5, 246.7). (d) To the middle level (M): (1007.2, 180.1, 251.0); (1028.5, 191.4, 226.0); (1036.0, 197.6, 233.5); (1024.7, 204.8, 233.5) (ledge climbs of 3 to 4); (1036.0, 207.8, 219.7), the plateau at y 207.8; (1058.5, 207.8, 237.2). The platform of locators 13, 14 and 17 (top 216.3 to 221.4; edge records from (1079.1, 239.5) to (1061.2, 261.2) and from (1059.0, 261.0) to (1056.8, 263.6), their outward normals facing south-west) is 8.8 above the plateau, so no jump reaches it. The pack's one movable block is the stair: it starts at (1050.82, 207.78, 258.82), size 3.98, height 2.83, step 1.99 per push, first axis +z and second axis +x, pushes available +z 2, +x 3, -z 4, -x 3. Push it 3 times toward +x (stand west of it; it ends at x 1056.80, top 210.6). Then stand on it, face north-east toward the edge near (1058.9, 261.0) and jump (Cross): the jump lifts the feet to 213.8, the edge top is 216.05 after the 0.25 allowance, 2.25 above the feet and inside the hang limit of 3.5, so the hero grabs the edge, hangs and pulls up onto (1061.0, 216.4, 262.2) (SPEC-0033 Behaviors 51 and 51A; the ground probe ignores the crate, so the grab is a hang, not a mantle). Locator 13 (1060.5, 216.8, 264.6) is 3 away; walk north-west across the platform to the lower volume at (1048.5, 217.3, 247.7), 28.8 wide, and press Square. (e) The top volume (optional, L): from (1061.0, 216.4, 262.2): (1088.5, 234.3, 224.7); panel at (1062.7, 241.9, 208.6) to (1057.2, 241.1, 203.5); panel at (1037.6, 257.1, 188.7) to (1038.5, 257.1, 184.7); (1022.2, 257.1, 182.2); (979.7, 257.1, 234.7), by locator 9 (977.7, 261.8, 246.7); then the ring up to (1022.2, 310.1, 203.5). The lower volume is enough. Resolved (the doors on the walk are in goal 425; a hero standing on a plate cannot use it, because the doors close about 1.2 s after he steps off and his way is 60 or more units long). Resolved (crates): the pack's three movable blocks are the plate weights. Block 0 starts at (-526.83, 0.93, -433.20): plate 6 is 12.5 north of it and plate 7 is 12.9 west and 12.5 north. Block 1 starts at (-276.49, 0.93, -644.14) beside plate 5, and block 2 at (-397.27, 1.03, -668.08) between plates 3 and 4. All three have size 2.0518, height 8.6086 and a step of 1.0259 per push; the first axis A is +z and the second axis B is +x. The pushes available (+A, +B, -A, -B) are 14, 5, 8, 31 for block 0; 14, 32, 10, 7 for block 1; 11, 26, 20, 21 for block 2. A block fires a plate volume while its position lies inside the plate rectangle widened by 0.6 times its size (1.23) and keeps firing while it rests there (SPEC-0048, Trigger volumes). Plan for plate 7 (rectangle corner (-536.2, -417.2), 7.0 along -x and 7.0 along -z, so widened x -544.4 to -535.0, z -425.4 to -416.0): stand east of block 0 and push it west 10 times (it ends at x -537.09, outside plate 6's widened x range of -531.9 to -522.5), then stand south of it and push it north 10 times (it ends at z -422.94). The minimum is 8 west + 8 north (16 moves); pushing north first fires plate 6, so push west before north. The record opens within about 1.2 s of the first fire, plays its camera once (1.0 s plus 1.5 s) and stays open while the crate rests there, so do not push the crate off. Blocks 1 and 2 are for records 2, 4 and 5 and are not needed. H for the block data; H for the plate rectangle axes (both extents 7.0); L for whether a resting block passes the plate script's shot and attack tests (a block fire carries no actor). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): after the 20 pushes, if no camera starts within 3 s, push one more step north, then one west, and wait 3 s after each; report the goal blocked after 120 s. |

The way is the Rope Gun (item 36, granted at the Farm; SPEC-0033 Behavior 51, state 27). The pack has two rope aim targets: target 1 at (404.62, -1542.58, -583.94), 14 above the foot, switched on by the tower door scene (level state 16, flag 317), and target 0 at (449.93, -1324.46, -624.42), high in the tower. Procedure on the arena floor: stand idle (state 1), push the right stick up or down to start the aim mode, turn the camera until target 1 passes the range test (camera eye to target strictly below 110) and the facing test (camera forward dot unit direction above 0.998), then hold R2 (Tor; for Roberta her R2 choice). The sight line from the eye lowered 1.5 is tested every 5 units against frame records and class-3 volumes, and the rim of the foot slab (outline top y -1556.8, 5.8 deep) blocks it from almost the whole floor: for camera eyes 4 to 8 above the feet and 8 to 20 behind, only spots within about 7 units of the point below the target stay clear, (406, -1614.6, -582) and (408, -1614.6, -578). The rope then reels the hero 0.8 per tick toward the rope target until the rope end is within 3.0 of the weapon point; the hero lets go and lands on the foot near (404, -1556.8, -584). Foot waypoints (x, y, z): (404.1, -1556.8, -575.3); (392.8, -1556.8, -577.8), the case trigger 0 cylinder at (393.4, -1556.8, -578.0), on the lift platform (x 383 to 404, z -589 to -568, whose lift is driven by the prompts). M for the rope procedure (the sight-line and eye assumptions), M for the order of the prompts; L for the foot geometry. Gaps settled in this list.
- Heroes: the Werelupe King fight is locked to Tor by the swap mode 0 that pack 1244's last scene sets (mode 2 returns at the King's death); Juppie in Act 3 is a Roberta fight (the dazed loop reads only her shots); the Faerie Sisters shield needs one hero shot (Roberta); the Plague Serpent, Black Knight, Undead Draik, Gelert and Ixi fights accept either hero.
- Puzzle orders: library 5, 6, 7, 8 with the entry scene first (777); the water-channel levers 7, 6, 7, 5 and the test button (1130); the three levers of 1249 in any order; the four crusher buttons within 18.4 s; the five Oubliette lever flags and the plates (1078); the three-level tower prompts (1096).
- Item sources: berries 5, 4 and 3 in the swamp (735); item 23 only from Guildmaster Marl; item 126 from pack 842 trigger 2; item 29 dropped by the Death Knight AI 10 of 1247 (starts at (-135.1, 19.4, -139.7)); item 18 dropped by the fifth Restless Spirit of 685 (starts at (-349.1, -12.2, -706.3), in the channel past the Oubliette ramp); item 103 given by the scene of 1081; item 117 in 1096.
- Positions settled: the witch Sophie at (-22.3, 0.0, 5.6) in 1251 (goal 370); Otto (-1044.8, 46.6, 1826.7) and Elspeth (-1085.6, 33.4, 1823.9) in 874 (goal 359; entity 6 is Otto, entity 7 is Elspeth); Rory the Shady Guy, start (392.4, 27.5, 697.7) in 1023 (goal 405); the Brightvale road guards on the road to teleport 0 of 880, loaded by the road rectangle or sphere (goal 336); the 1079 gate walls inside their trigger volumes (goal 429); the cell exits 1098 to 1121 to 1122 (goals 443 and 444); the Oubliette entrance as a gate trap with the key found inside (goals 421 to 424); the Rope Gun as the only way up the glade shaft (goal 397).

Open items and defaults.
- Resolved: the entity positions of the interior talkers (witch in 1251, miners in 874, cloak seller in 1023) are in the rows of goals 370, 359 and 405. Pack 685 has no breakables and the 12 breakables of 1081 drop only empty placements, so no breakable matters in either pack; the items come from characters (goals 421 and 432).

Resolved: the guard group trigger of 880 (goal 336), the item-29 source (goal 375), the item-18 source (goal 421), the cell exits of 1098, 1121 and 1122 (goals 443, 444, 448) and the rope route of 956 (goal 397: the rope is required, order 2, 1, 0).
- Resolved (Medium; goals 420, 421, 425, 426): the Oubliette needs the crate puzzle, not a held plate. The corridor gaps of 685 are bridged by thin solid slabs on both rails (one hop with a running jump; no jump alone crosses the 20.2 wide gap, whose reach is 14.0); at the first visit all six door records are closed and the platform side reaches only the first rooms; push crate 0 west and north onto plate 7 (record 0), then take the route of goal 421 to the channel, pull lever 26 (mandatory, it opens door B for the way back) and return by the route of goal 425. The second hero cannot hold a plate (the parked hero is not in the world). Remaining doubts: the plate rectangle axes, whether a resting crate passes the plate script's tests, and the standing behaviour on the 0.35 thick slabs. The gate-trap reading of goals 422 to 424 (object poses) is Medium.

The order of the 1096 tower case triggers is fixed by the arming words and the heights; the join of the tower foot's lift platform to the prompt ledge and the Rope Gun chain of goal 397 stay Medium-Low.
- Whether trigger 12 of pack 956 (goal 398) or the fight is reached first depends on the order the heroes walk; the rows give the fight first.

## Goal list for an automated player (Act 4)

Purpose: the ordered goals for walkthrough steps 44 to 46, from the return to pack 1300 after movie 54 (the end of the Act 3 list, goal 450) through the rescue of Queen Fyora in pack 1428, the Act 4 start, the four Altador shrines, the waking of Jerdana in pack 1382, the Darkest Faerie fight in pack 1432, the finale, the epilogue in pack 1300 and the credits. Same table form, kinds, positions and confidence tags as the Act 1 list (read its "How to read the table" first). Goals are numbered from 500, so they do not clash with Act 1 (1 to 99), Act 2 (100 to 155) or Act 3 (300 to 452). Confidence is per row (H High, M Medium, L Low); a row marked M or L names the link that is not settled.

Act 4 conventions.
- Row fields. Every row's notes open with three short fields. "H:" names the hero that must be active ("either" means no restriction; Tor is the default pick when a hero is needed for melee and Roberta when one is needed for shots). "SW:" names every hero switch in the row, by the player (L1 and R1 chord) or by the script ("none" when there is none). "AR:" names the arena entry and exit when the row starts, runs in or leaves a closed space (a boss room, a shrine pack or a trial room), and reads "none" otherwise.
- Added kinds. SWITCH: press the L1 and R1 chord to change the active hero; it works only while the swap mode allows it (SPEC-0053, section 3.1). SHOOT: fire the active hero's quick shots (Roberta) at a named target; each hit that counts pays the magic cost (SPEC-0036, Magic). STRIKE: stand inside a named cylinder and make a melee attack (Square) while the hero's body overlaps the named box.
- Rectangle triggers are given by reference corner (x, y, z), the two horizontal or in-plane extents, and the direction of the first extent. Sphere and cylinder triggers are given by centre or base and radius. Teleport rectangles ("teleport" below) are entered by standing inside and pressing Square at the context 13 prompt, except where a row says contact is enough.
- Heroes. The Act 4 start (goal 514) leaves swap mode 2 (free) with the selected hero unchanged and heals both heroes (SPEC-0053, section 3.7), so the player may switch freely except where a row names a scripted lock: pack 1428 locks to Roberta (mode 1) only if she goes down before the shot meter completes, and ends the game if Tor is selected at that moment (goal 503); pack 1394 sets mode 3 and switches the hero by script at each lever (goals 543 to 546); pack 1432 sets mode 3 at the fight entry and swaps by script (goals 577 to 583). Hero 0 is Tor and hero 1 is Roberta in the scripts.
- Walking chains (pack: ground neighbours). 1300: 1344. 1344: 1300, 1335, 1428. 1428: 1344. 1411: 1391 (cells); 1411 to 1363 is a script load (goal 520). 1363: 1370. 1370: 1353, 1350, 1358, 1363. 1391: 1411, 1381. 1381: 1391. 1353 to 1392 is a script load (goal 539). 1392: 1395, 1353. 1395: 1392, 1393. 1393: 1395, 1396. 1396: 1393, 1394. 1350 to 1371, 1358 to 1397, 1363 to 1398 and 1391 to 1382 are script loads. 1432 is entered only by the return world that movie 72 sets. High for the neighbour lists; the exact crossing inside each pair is Medium.
- Items that matter. Item 117 is taken and item 116 is given by the pack 1428 entry scene. Items 25, 26 and 27 (mosaic pieces) are world pickups in pack 1411 (goals 516 to 518). Elemental Motes 160 to 171 are world pickups in the Altador districts (goal 562) and may already be held. Item 172 (Nova Mote) answers every orb colour and is dropped by the final fight's Minion Warriors. Item 50 (Potion of Revival) is optional.

The other two Wizards fire homing or straight shots and never summon. Wave 2's Wizards are variants 1 and 2 (rows 212, 213) and never summon. Kill the fire Wizard first to stop the Grunts. The door for the ground floor at the same x and z is teleport 5 (height 7.7); it works only once flag 695 is set (the Treasury's own second exit sets it) and is not the story door. Resolved route from the hub arrival to the ground floor under the door (654 units, no step above 2.9): west to (-92, 68), down the spiral stair through (-108, 52) height 27.7, (-108, 16) height 18.1, (-128, 4) height 15 and (-132, 52) height 4.8 to (-144, 60) height 0 (the hand-over from pack 1370 to pack 1358), then (-324, 60), (-384, 0) and (-528, 0) at floor height 6.9. | 534 | WALK | 1397 | Trigger 12: box corner (377.9, -50.7, -325.0), extents 10.0 along x by 17.7 (height 10.2). Walk in: movie 63 plays, 102 and 349 are set and twelve item 139 pickups appear at records 24 to 35. | 700 on | 102 on | H: either. SW: none. AR: inside. The scripts do not describe the route; the floor shows it in part. The room has four tiers: the start room (heights 0 to 22, x 0 to 152; the arrival plate at height 17.3 hangs above its floor, whose record 1 is (2.9, 1.2, 0.0)), the button and statue hall (x 72 to 628, z -144 to 152, heights -9 to 33), the lower halls (x 340 to 492, z -268 to 24, heights -50 to -22) and the box platform (x 340 to 436, z -344 to -300, height -50). The tiers are not joined by steps of up to 3.2. The objects raised by the buttons (9, 10 and 11) carry no floor or wall data, so they do not join the start room to the hall. The start room (plate and floor together) reaches x 154 and no further in every state of the lifts, the seesaw objects 0 to 3 and the grate, with walks, falls, ledge climbs of up to 12 and running jumps of up to 14 all allowed. Join start room to hall (NOT settled, Low): the module has no hero move, door or teleport between the two (its hero moves are the two arrival spots, the button, lever and statue spots and the trigger 12 spot; its two door callbacks are the west doors that leave for pack 1358), no rope-gun target exists in the pack, and no wall gate bit is ever set. The start room is closed on the hall side by one-sided tall walls that belong to the pack's own collision: a wall across x 152.5 (z -40.5 to 40.5, heights 14.0 to 46.9) in front of an 11.5-wide void (x 154.8 to 166.3) before the hall's edge at height 16.9, and two round bays (x 98 to 113, z +-41 to +-50) at the same heights. They face into the start room, so a hero coming from the hall is not stopped by them. Without those walls the ring walkway (height 16.0) runs straight on into the hall corridors at z about +46 and -46 (height 16.9, about 130 units of walk, no climb), so that is where the join lies. The one other ground touching both areas is a raised gantry (height 32.9, x 72 to 99, z 25 to 97) that ends above the start room's central pillar and ramps down into the hall near z 93 to 135; it is 14.5 above the highest ground the start room reaches and the pillar below it (a type 3 wall to 18.6, crown at 26.5) cannot be climbed. Why static data cannot settle it: nothing in the module changes those walls, yet the game must let the hero out, so either a hero-collision rule that lets the hero through the walls is not modelled or a jump route past the pillar needs a physical test. From the hall's east end the route to the box is resolved: about 525 units (west along z -36 at height -7 from (588, -36) to (508, -34), one fall of 17.4 near (490, -20) to height -24.5, then (440, -10) at -34.8, (400, -14) at -36.1, down the ramp to (400, -54) at -44.9 and (382, -90) at -50.1, north along x 378 to 396 to (396, -284), (396, -324) and the box). The two dips (and a third near z -164) of the earlier route are not climbs of 12: each dip holds a lift platform (objects 29, 30 and 31, 15 wide, spanning the whole dip, z -180 to -148, -240 to -208 and -301 to -269) that stands at about -60.6 and rises 10.48 to about -50.1, level with the hall (-50.1), when its block plate is fired (Resolved, High: the plate scene plays the lift animation; see below). The block plates are script triggers 11, 10 and 9 (boxes 3.1 tall with horizontal sides of 1.0 to 1.6 at (366.3, -51.6, -106.1), (406.6, -51.6, -197.8) and (366.6, -51.6, -258.4)), which fire when a movable block comes within 2.954 of the footprint (block starts and push counts in SPEC-0048). Resolved (High): what raises the lifts. Each plate runs a lift scene, but only for one block: plate 11 for block 3, plate 10 for block 1, plate 9 for block 2, once each and one scene at a time; a hero touching a plate, or another block, does nothing and uses nothing up. The scene takes control of the hero and the three guards, plays camera track 2, 3 or 4, starts two effects, waits 1 s and then 2 s, plays the lift animation at 0.2 speed (lift 29 for plate 11, lift 30 for plate 10, lift 31 for plate 9: about 6.3 s for lift 29 and 6.7 s for the others), keeps the hero for 7 s more, stops the effects, and for plates 11 and 10 swaps the invisible block-floor object (plate 11 shows object 33; plate 10 hides it and shows object 34) and re-places the three blocks on the floor then drawn; it ends about 1 s after the camera returns. With flag 102 already set at load the lifts start raised and the plates are not registered. Resolved (Medium): plate 9 can be reached. The plates must be used in the order 11, 10, 9, because at load the blocks stand on the pack's own floor entry, which ends at z -149.5, before the first dip; object 33's floor (outline to z -210.1) lets block 1 cross the first dip, and object 34's floor (outline to z -270.8) lets block 2 cross the second. Plate 11: block 3 in 3 pushes toward -z. Plate 10: block 1 in 47 moves (4 west, 32 south, 11 east) once plate 11 has fired. Plate 9: block 2 in 82 moves (12 west, 45 south, 1 east, 15 south, 4 west, 5 south, ending near (370.0, -256.4)) once plate 10 has fired. The floor objects cover the lift columns at the hall level (x 363 to 378, 379.5 to 395 and 394 to 409), so after plate 11 the hero can cross the first dip, and after plate 10 the second; the walk stops in the third dip (z -301 to -269) until plate 9 raises lift 31. With the lifts down and no plate fired the walk stops at z -148, at the first dip, and a hero cannot climb out of a dip (a hang reaches 6.7). Stage 4 then needs six passes through trigger 34, a 2.2 by 2.2 column (x -0.69 to 1.51, z 1042.41 to 1044.61, from height 6.8 up, centre 0.405, 1043.506): the trigger refuses the hero, so a Harris must walk into it; each pass removes the Harris that fired it and the count rises by 1. | 722 on | 723 on (count 6); region 1401 (the Whinnies) loads | H: either. SW: none. AR: inside. Resolved (R-0223; Medium: computed from the enemy rules, not played): the firer is one of the Evil Harrises themselves (the module tests entities 16 to 21 against the trigger volumes every tick), so no hero action fires trigger 34: it refuses every hero contact, shot and blow, and a hero standing in the column blocks a Harris's fire as well. The column is 4.2 by 4.2 for a Harris (x -1.69 to 2.50, z 1041.41 to 1045.60 at its body radius 1.0), tall enough that the Harrises' feet height of 9.6 is inside it. The Harrises start at x +30.4 and -30.6, z 1076.7, 1051.7 and 1024.3, height 9.6, wandering. Plan for an automated player: (1) walk into the arena (the arm) and let the Harrises notice the hero; once one fights, all six come; (2) face one Harris and keep it between the hero and the column's centre on one straight line, at most about 14 units from the column, then either run at it from under 7 units so that it backs straight away (15.9 units per second) into the column, or strike it once with the sword from about 2.5 units so that it slides about 14.6 units straight away from the hero; stay out of the column; (3) a pass is about 11 to 14 s of scene; the firer is removed for good and the other five are put on the ring of points round the arena; repeat with the next Harris until the count is 6 (six Harrises, six passes). Do not leave the shrine area before the count is 6: the removals can be remembered while the count restarts at 0. A Harris's own swing is refused for the firing test, and it swings only when the hero faces away, so face it. Worked sequences are in SPEC-0043 (stage 4). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): as above; if no pass happens within 180 s, move to 20 units from the nearest Harris and try again; report blocked after 600 s without a new pass. The Drackonacks of region 1399 take no damage in this pack; do not fight them. L |

Gaps settled in this list.
- Heroes: the shot meter of goal 508 needs Roberta (only she fires hero shots). The final fight is Tor for the three stuns and Roberta for the three shots and the landing, switched by script under swap mode 3; the player cannot switch in the arena. Pack 1394's four levers switch the hero by script in the order Roberta, Tor, Roberta, Tor, whichever order the levers are used in.
- Items 25, 26 and 27 are world pickups on the upper terraces of pack 1411 (goals 516 to 518). The six elemental Motes needed for the pedestals are world pickups in packs 1411, 1363, 1350 and 1358 (goal 562).
- Pack 1371 branch B (the wave trial) is entered from teleport 2 of pack 1350, not through the arena master; its exit counts as a won arena round.
- The Altador statue puzzles: pack 1353 needs 2, 6 and 7 presses; pack 1397 needs 0, 3, 2 and 1 presses.
- The pack 1394 lever course needs no particular lever order; each lever switches the hero by script.
- Pack 1382 is reached from pack 1391 by the lift and the volume that sets the next world; pack 1381 is optional (Resolved: no code outside pack 1381 reads flags 708 to 719, and the lift is entered from pack 1411).
- Resolved: after movie 54 the hero stands at record 1 of pack 1300 (value 50 for world 151). The walk to Queen Fyora's chamber is one straight line north along x 0: over the garden floor of pack 1300 to its north tip at about (0, -232), then, after the world swap, on the flat floor of pack 1344 (height 7.4) to pack 1428's corridor at (0, -270).
- Resolved: Altador is one x-z frame. The hub plateau (height 30) at the origin drops by spiral stairs to the Arena (north), Bazaar (east) and Money (west) floors, and the park is joined to the plateau on the south; the waypoints are in goals 521, 522, 530, 536 and 550.
- Resolved: the Coliseum waves are wave 1 (region 1378): one Brute, three Warriors, two Grunts; wave 2 (region 1379): one Brute, one Warrior, two Wizards, two Magicians; wave 3 (region 1380): three Brutes, three Wizards, and up to four Grunts that Wizards lob.
- Resolved: the Mortog yard's zone centre is locator 6, (0.0, -0.7, 361.7), the plate's own point; locator 7 gives the radius 5.2.
- Resolved: the stage-4 firer in pack 1398 is an Evil Harris, never the hero (the Whinnies are not loaded while stage 4 is armed).

Open items and defaults.
- Resolved: the crossings 1300 to 1344 to 1428 and the Altador hub routes (goals 501, 502, 521, 522, 530, 536, 550) are given as floor waypoints. Still open: whether a world swap accepts the 1500-unit change of height between the garden of pack 1300 and the floor of pack 1344 (Medium; Default: walk north along x 0 until the garden floor ends and carry on), and the climb to the Treasury door slab in pack 1358 (re-checked with walls, ledge climbs and jumps: the slab and its arched strip are an island 17 units from the end of the nearest road at height 22, a strafe jump falls 1 to 2 units short, and nothing in the pack or its script changes this; Low; Default: path-find to (-528, 0), then try the arched strip beside the slab, and report blocked after 60 s).
- Resolved in part: the floor route through packs 1392, 1395, 1393 and 1396 into pack 1394 is given (goals 540 to 542, about 1,300 units, one hand-over per pack). Still open (Low): gates, levers and guard rooms (they are not in the floor samples); Default: follow the waypoints, press the nearest unpressed lever when blocked, step on a room plate when a guard door blocks. In pack 1394 the floor from record 0 joins only lever S1. Resolved: each lever moves the hero to the next leg's start (records 1, 15, 16, then 8), so the later levers are reached from those spots, not from record 0; the sinking platform has no floor data. Legs A (to S1) and E (record 8 to the exit trigger) are confirmed with walls; legs B, C and D (S3, S2, S4) are not (Low; Default: follow the lever order 15, 16, 18, 17 with jumps and falls allowed and report blocked after 90 s; the countdown resets the leg).
- Resolved in part: the route from the pack 1397 statue hall to trigger 12 is a walk of about 525 units ending at (388, -316) once the three dip lifts (objects 29, 30, 31, each 10.48 high, raised by the block plates, triggers 11, 10 and 9) are up; the dips are not climbs of 12. Resolved: the plates raise the lifts, each for one block (plate 11 block 3, plate 10 block 1, plate 9 block 2, used in that order), and all three blocks can reach their plates (3, 47 and 82 moves; goal 534). Still open: the join from the start room to the hall (the pack's own tall one-sided walls close the start room; the ring walkway would run on into the hall corridors at z about +-46 without them; the objects the buttons raise carry no floor data) (Low; Default: path-find with the vault walls treated as passable from the start room toward the hall, falls allowed). The climb from the lower plaza of pack 1411 to its upper terrace is not joined by the floor, walls and objects modelled (Low; Default: path-find with falls and ledge climbs allowed, report blocked after 60 s).
- Resolved: the Coliseum waves and the Mortog zone centre (see Gaps settled). Resolved: of the three wave-3 Wizards only the one with variant 0 (row 211, on the ring point (-0.1, 7.2, -26.5)) lobs Grunts, up to four live at once; the other two (rows 213 and 216) and both wave-2 Wizards never summon (goal 528; High for the one summoner, Medium for the timing); Default: kill the fire Wizard first, then every placed Minion, then every Grunt that appears.
- Resolved (R-0223; Medium): stage 4 of pack 1398 is fired by an Evil Harris, not the hero. A Harris reaches the column by wandering, by backing away from a hero who closes in, or by a sword knock-back of about 14.6 units; the hero must line up hero, Harris and column and attack or close in (see goal 556). The old default of standing still and not attacking is dropped. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): the goal 556 plan, blocked after 600 s without a pass.
- Resolved: the stations and blocks of pack 1381 are optional (goal 565).

## Edge cases and error handling

- In a game begun at start index 0, rows whose done flag is never set stay in the journal for the rest of the game once started (some of these flags are set by the stage table for start indices 1 to 8; SPEC-0049): row 145 (done 341) and row 152 (done 352). Rows whose start flag is never set are never listed: row 146 (start 341), row 153 (start 352), row 129 (start 268), rows 117 and 118 (start 323 and 324); pack 1428 sets row 118's done flag 326 anyway. Row 48 (the lunge lesson, flag 182) is never completed. Medium-High (SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags).
- Rows 42 to 46 (five nest rows) share start flag 174 and have done flags 625 to 629. Pack 1056's breakable routine sets them, one flag per nest breakable as each is broken, and advances the 175 to 179 chain (SPEC-0044, 1056), so the rows close as the nests are broken. Medium-High for 625 to 628; Medium for 629 (the fifth breakable).
- The farm counter: each activation of pack 909 clears 107, 108, 109 and 110 whenever any of the four is off. A finished round-up (all four on) is kept; any partial round-up is reset on every entry. High.
- Pack 1428 has no once-per-game latch on the trap scene: reloading 1428 with 322 on and touching trigger 4 replays movie 55 and the item swap. Medium.
- The sewer-drain counter in pack 1171 computes its flag as 162 + n, where n (1 to 5) is the number of drains cleared, so it sets 163 to 167 in turn, including 166 and 167 (rows 37 and 38). High.
- Flags 592 and 593 belong to side-quest rows 228 and 229 (the traitor knight), not to the main story (SPEC-0049).
- Start points (SPEC-0035) set the stage rows but do not move the hero, so a stage start begins in the default packs with the flags of a later point in the story.
- **Thieves' crew in 1139 (R-0148; High for the script and the idle rule, Medium-High for the design reading).** Pack 1139 places five characters, in entity order 0 to 4: Heermedjet (the thief of the hand-over; monster type 39, variant 1), Meerouladen (type 39, variant 0), Gil the Fence (type 72, row 152), Drool (type 72, row 152) and Lump (type 71, row 151). The last three are class-17 rows with the hostile word set, but none is meant to be fought. (1) What keeps them idle: the placement's default AI mode word is 0 on all five (no path, no wander size, alarm group byte 255, no drop, not summon-reserved). In class-17 state 0 the per-tick step only re-applies that default word, and the notice check (the row's sense band, awareness value and view cone) runs only in states 3, 5, 6, 7 and 9. So the three never notice the hero at any range and their agro range is never evaluated. No script, entity call or alarm moves them: the 1139 module makes no AI, agro, walk, scene, camera or death call on any entity. Only a hit moves one into the agro state; it then fights (row 152: 50 hit points, strafe approach; row 151: 60 hit points, rush approach) and no story step reacts. Thieves of the same rows elsewhere use default modes 5, 7 or 8. (2) What stops the hero: no wall, placed object or movable block does. Pack 1139 has no movable block, its one placed object stands at the origin, and the nearest collision shape to each body centre is 8.4 units for Drool (a cylinder), 5.7 for Gil (a cylinder) and 4.2 for Lump (a post of radius 1.24 at (51.1, -9.9)). The solid thing is each character's own body: an upright cylinder of the monster row's radius (2.0 for rows 151 and 152), against the hero's 1.175. The shared body separation (SPEC-0033, bodies pushing the hero) pushes the hero out to a centre separation of about 3.1 to 3.2 (radius sum 3.175), not 4.5. A hero held at about 4.5 is held by something the original does not have (the body sum would have to be 4.5, for example a hero radius of 2.5). (3) Talk: a class-17 character is not on the talker list (townsfolk only), but it is on the lock-on list while alive and not hidden (range 20.0 for Tor, 100.0 for Roberta, facing cosine at least 0.5), and the talk entry is chosen by the candidate's entity id, not by its class. With the entry registered (entities 0 to 4 all are) and the 3-D distance below 5.8, the "Talk" caption shows and Square starts the callback, so the standing range for Gil, Drool and Lump is 3.2 to 5.8 (Medium-High; not played end to end). (4) Script: Init registers the five talk entries and nothing else touches them. Gil (entity 2): the one-time sale of SPEC-0039, then comments by flag. Drool (entity 3): grunts, lines 1 to 3, lines 4 to 6 with flag 305 and lines 7 to 9 with flag 319. Lump (entity 4): wishes of luck with the treasure, lines 37 to 39, lines 40 to 42 with flag 305 and lines 43 to 45 with flag 319. None of the three sets a flag, joins a scene or movie, or starts a later fight; flag 678 is set and tested only by entities 0 and 1. (5) Story: the player has no task with them except to buy from Gil (a potion or a Silver Negg, SPEC-0039) and to hear them; the progress step is Heermedjet. Killing one changes no story state; a dead character is no longer a talk candidate, so Gil's sale would be gone (Medium). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): build the three as idle talkers in state 0 that never notice the hero, are hittable and enter agro when hit, with body radii 1.175 and 2.0.

## Test vectors

Synthetic, hand-checkable.

1. Act start 3 on an all-off bitmap: flag 248 on, swap mode 2, Tor selected. Rows whose start flag is 248 become listable.
2. Quest rows 0 to 3 (start and done flags 107/108, 108/109, 109/110, 110/111): set 107, and row 0 is listed; set 108, and row 0 is gone and row 1 is listed; set 109, and row 2 is listed; set 110, and row 3 is listed.
3. Pack 1411 with 335 on and 336 off: movie 69 plays and flags 336 and 97 turn on. With 337 off nothing further plays and pack 1432 is not entered.
4. Pack 1300 entered with entry message 80 after the finale (94 and 365 on): flag 366 turns on and the hero is placed at landing 2. Trigger 2 then plays movie 79 and sets 367; trigger 3 plays movie 83 and sets 368; the next play pass opens the credits, and their end tears the world down.
5. Text lookup (synthetic table): a table block at file offset 0x2000 and a row whose +0x10 field holds 0x0180 give the objective text starting at file offset 0x2180 and running to the first zero byte. A row with scene id 3 names the area whose table-5 row 3 text offset is resolved the same way.
6. Row kind: a row with +0x00 = 1 is a side quest; the story test skips it.
7. Pack 1382 with five pedestals lit: nothing. Light the sixth with a matching Mote equipped: 98 and 337 turn on, movie 70 plays once, and the hero is returned to 1411, where movie 71 can then play (337 on, 338 off). Pedestal check: a Fire Mote (160) on the pedestal at trigger 21 lights it; a Bubble Mote (162) there drops Mote 160 at locator 6.
8. Pack 909 update with 707 on and 1 off (706 either way): flag 1 on, 706 off, movie queue [88, 1], act start 1.
9. Pack 1327 library door with 240 on, 245 off and 223 already on: 223 unchanged; 245, 246, 247, 400 and 397 on; movie queue [27, 29, 90, 30]; return world 64.
10. Pack 777 type-10 inventory: three groups contain six ladder-face/top records and no climb panels; the nearest horizontal record segment is about 68.43 units from trigger index 8. This establishes the authored ladder/panel layout, not a route to the trigger.
10. Pack 1129 activated with world-109 entry message 10: hero at locator 1, long fall, 305 on, message reset to -1. With message 20: hero at locator 0, 305 unchanged.
11. Pack 1428 with 322 on and 330 on: touching trigger 0 sets 331; the next control step then queues [56, 91, 57, 58] and sets 333.
12. Pack 1174 entered from 1098: flag 218 on. The hero touches the trigger 1 rectangle: flags 219 and 220 turn on, movie 16 plays, act start 2 runs (Roberta only, swap mode 1), then movie 89, flag 221, return world eW 151 and movie 18; the next area is pack 1300. Touching the section rectangles first (triggers 2, 3 and 4) changes no story flag.
13. Step 41 gate: flag 304 off, the cloak owned (step 37) but not put on, 1,200 Neopoints. Talking to entity 72 in 1023 does not offer the 1,000 Neopoints payment, and flag 13 stays off. After Square on the Items tab (the active hero now wears item 126) and no attack or hit before the talk, the payment is offered; yes removes 1,000 Neopoints and sets 13.

14. Pack 909 item-placement check: the direct Empty Bucket point is at (-1905.838, 39.937, 1159.590), and the direct Pliers point is at (-1934.064, 74.167, 1103.691); the pump interaction converts item 6 to item 7, which Mum consumes.

15. Pack 909 initial no-stage activation: the fourth character slot loads region 910 while the round-up is incomplete; the other three slots load regions 914, 920 and 926, placing Lucy, Patricia and Hubert at their listed farm coordinates.

16. Pack 909 region 910 placement check: the first active farm set contains three Whinnies, five Flower Monsters, and the named region records Lucy, Patricia and Hubert from slots P, Q and R (regions 914, 920 and 926).
