19. Farm chore dialogue chain: with flag 110 on, Patricia's talk sets 111 and selects line 49. Pump confirm succeeds only with 111 on, 112 off and item 6 held; it consumes 6, grants 7 and sets 112. Patricia's return talk consumes 7, sets 113 and shows line 45. Hubert's next talk sets 114 and shows line 101; his talk with item 34 held consumes it, sets 116 and 117, and shows line 97.

20. Farm-road hero staging: pack 691 locator 19 is (-1626.268, 48.256, 1125.632), and locator 3 is (-1600.597, 49.476, 1105.347). The intro moves Tor to locator 19, then the fight starts after moving him to locator 3.

21. Plain New Game start index 0: wallet 0; inventory contains item 119 x1, item 150 x3 and item 149 x3; no Golden Negg remains. Tor starts at 150/150 health and 90/90 magic; Roberta at 120/120 health and 150/150 magic.

22. Pack 1023 chase route: locator 477 to 484 are the eight Meerca wait points; locator 485 is the catch point. Their coordinates are listed in step 6. For each of the first eight, the Meerca waits until Tor is within 30 units before setting the matching flag 128 to 135 and continuing.

23. First house entry: pack 951 entry record 0 is (15.273, 0.974, 16.665); on first entry with flag 120 clear, its story thread sets 120, plays FMV 2 and grants item 15.

24. Pack 951 chest check: its kind-1 group 0 is locked by item 15/message 124 and uses locator 15; with item 15, Square removes the key, opens the chest and drops reward placement 1, whose point is item 98 (Wooden Sword) at (-14.978, 14.097, -23.937).

25. First Farm Road save point: pack 691 embedded save-point record 0 is at (-1706.435, 45.558, 1193.245). In normal play its sound range is below 15 units and Save Game prompt range below 5 units; Square opens the save screen when the hero is not busy.

26. Farm spawn selectors: region 910 has three Whinnies of type 109, variant 0, key 2335, and five Flower Monsters of type 69, variant 0, key 619; see the positions in step 3.

27. First ambush HP check: selected rows are Darrick 153/80 HP, Sid 151/60 HP and Drub 152/50 HP, starting sum 190. A cumulative loss of 30 leaves the watcher waiting; 31 ends the fight scene.

28. Post-fight actor: pack 691 entity 10 maps to region 695 record 1, a type 72 variant 0 Drub at (-1572.542, 49.308, 1105.604). The post-FMV scene activates him at 25% HP and enables aggression.

29. Pack 909 Act 1 start: TriggerActStart(1) sets swap mode 0, selects Tor and performs no heal; Roberta cannot be selected during this opening lock.

30. Opening tutorial controls: Lucy flag-107 exchange selects the movement hint (left stick move, right stick look, hold R1 to run); flag 110 selects the area-map hint after a 1-second delay (directional-pad Left).

31. Pack 1217 Act 1 Juppie staging: intro hero locators 88 to 90 are listed in step 9; the Tentacle stage spawn targets are 13 to 18, all at arena floor y = 0. Spawn 1 uses 13, spawn 2 uses 14 and 15, spawn 3 uses 16 to 18.

32. Act 1 Juppie placement: pack 1217 entity 7 resolves to region 1218 record 0, type 6 variant 0 key 1615, at (4.311, 1.911, 25.921).

33. Act 1 Juppie HP thresholds: type 6 variant 0 selects row 7 with 190 max HP. Threshold 5/6 is crossed at 158 HP or lower; threshold 1/2 at 95 HP or lower.

34. Pack 1023 Meerca actor: unfinished chase loads region 1024; record 0 is Meerouladen, type 39 variant 0 key 4032, authored at (-34.942, 29.382, 634.237), then moved to the first waypoint whose flag 128 to 135 is clear.

35. Farm outstanding-tasks hint: on the New Game path, flag 302 is clear and flag 110 is clear until all three Whinnies are penned; before then Patricia's routine cycles lines 50, 51 and 52. Once 110 is set, her first eligible talk shows line 49 and sets 111; later talks can show 46, then 189, then 47 and 48, after which 46, 47 and 48 cycle (SPEC-0045, pack 909). The hint can therefore appear during the initial farm visit after the roundup. When flag 302 is set later in step 39, the higher-priority branch selects lines 0 to 5 instead, so the earlier later-return claim for line 189 does not hold. High for the selector branches and line sequence; no live-game display check.

36. First road ambush staging: points 23, 22 and 21 are the first walk goals for Drub, Sid and Darrick, and points 27, 26 and 25 are their later scene positions, as listed in step 5. Facing vectors are stored with each entry pose.

37. Pack 909 after the farm chores: flag 114 scene uses Hubert entity 44 from region 927 at points 15 and 16; flag 117 scene uses the same placement through points 16 and 15, then a separate Hubert entity 45 from region 928 through points 21 and 23. Their target positions are listed in step 3.

38. Act 1 Juppie spawn points 13 to 18 all face (0, 0, 1), as listed in step 9.

39. First house entry: pack 951 loads region 953; Patricia, Lucy and Hubert placements are listed in step 4.

40. Pack 909 initial character orientations: the three Whinnies, three farm-family actors and five Flower Monsters use the facing vectors listed with their positions in step 3.

41. Act 1 Juppie actor: region 1218 record 0 begins at the position and facing listed in step 9; the Act 1 intro animates the actor without changing that authored position.

42. Pack 1217 Act 1 fight gate: flag 145 on, flag 82 off and marker 83 off selects the fight; a fresh button-5 press at intro start skips the cinematic but retains the first Tentacle spawn.
43. Farm pump and quest-item pickup: the pump Square prompt comes from trigger index 5, cylinder record 1, a vertical cylinder based at (-1949.410, 45.470, 1058.061), length 4.493 and radius 2.663; its center is about (-1949.410, 47.716, 1058.061). A pack-909 ground-patch candidate at the cylinder center samples y=45.335, about 0.134 below the cylinder base. The Empty Bucket point likewise has a ground-patch candidate at y=39.924, about 0.013 below its placement y. These two local surface anchors do not establish a continuous walkable route between the bucket and pump. The callback requires flag 111 on, flag 112 off and item 6 held before running the pump exchange. The Empty Bucket (item 6) faces (0.937, 0, 0.350); the Pliers (item 34) face (-0.021, 0.151, 0.988). Their placed copies have infinite lifetime, so despite their important-item text flag they use the normal 2.675-unit pickup distance, not the 25-unit age-based pickup rule (SPEC-0021; SPEC-0045).
44. Act 1 Juppie start volume: pack 1217 kind-7 rectangle record 0 has flag 0x0100, trigger index 0 and no gate mask; its reference point, extents, in-plane axes and normal are listed under pack 1217 in SPEC-0045. Only the Act 1 fight-due branch registers the trigger, and selected-hero body contact starts the intro (or its button-5 skip). After the button-5 skip the hero is not moved from the start-volume crossing, about 50.9 units from the boss.
45. Farm Pliers access boundary: the item point is (-1934.064, 74.167, 1103.691). A pack-909 ladder face spans vertically from about y=55.865 to 73.441, with its center about 48.6 horizontal units from the item. The placement and ladder records alone do not establish a continuous walkable route or pickup from below.
46. Farm bucket-to-pump surface check: the Empty Bucket placement at (-1905.838, 39.937, 1159.590) has a pack-909 ground-patch candidate at y=39.924; the pump-cylinder center at (-1949.410, 47.716, 1058.061) has a candidate at y=45.335, with the cylinder base at y=45.470. Along the straight x/z segment between those points, 401 equally spaced static samples each have at least one linked ground-patch candidate. At t=0.1, cell (22, 89) yields owner-0 entry 139 at y=39.359 after the patch-list checks; across all samples, candidate records vary and heights range about 39.36 to 75.65. This does not establish which surface the moving hero follows, continuous support between samples, collision clearance or a walkable route.

## Open questions

Questions 1 to 14 have resolved story-route answers, with the remaining geometry and reachability caveats stated in each entry. Question 1 retains one geometry point, and question 12 retains a reachability part. The clean side must not fill open gaps by inspecting the original.

1. Resolved: flag 337 (the step that wakes Jerdana is done) is set, with flag 98, in pack 1382 once all six elemental pedestals are lit; each is lit by a weapon strike with a Mote of its element equipped, lit pedestals stay lit for the rest of the load, and the order is free (SPEC-0041). Movie 70 plays and the hero returns to 1411. The chain is 1411 -> 1391 -> 1381 -> 1391 -> 1382 -> 1411, then movies 71 and 72, then the final fight in 1432 (step 46; SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags). The two 1381 groups (flags 708 to 719) are described in SPEC-0042: a station strike sets 708 + k, and movable block k pushed onto its pad spot sets 714 + k. The load from 1391 into 1382 does not wait on them in code: 1391's trigger 1 needs only the hero's contact (High). Still open: physical reachability before the 1381 block group is finished. Pack 1411 names pack 1391 as a streamed neighbor in cells with exit values 1–4; this is not a fixed arrival point. When the ground probe transfers the current-world role to the streamed neighbor, it checks the hero's same x/z position, with no per-pack coordinate transform. At the seam sample (x=0,z=190), the 1411 height sample is about y=149.6. This x/z position is inside 1391's long-lift rest height patch, whose bounds are approximately x=[−39,49], z=[187,275]; the patch samples about y=0.07 there. Trigger 2 is centered near (−0.02,220.55), also over the lift patch. These facts locate the lift surface relative to the streamed seam but do not show a safe landing or traversable connection. A static scan found no top-level type-11 sphere, cylinder or finite rectangle intersecting the tested vertical segment at x=0,z≈196.003 from y≈0.07 to 149.628; the nearest was the lift-trigger cylinder in pack 1391, whose surface is about 4.55 units from the segment. This narrows only that tested line: the roughly 149.5-unit drop, collision opening/order elsewhere and lift behavior during arrival remain unresolved. Reach from the lift to trigger 1, and any dependence of intervening blockers on the six block placements, also remain unproven (SPEC-0049 OQ26). Packs 1411, 1391 and 1381 each have an empty type-10 table, so none provides an authored ladder or climb panel for this route (SPEC-0006, Type 10); this does not rule out other movement or collision paths.
2. Resolved except three flags: every main-story flag the story tests has an ordinary level-script setter (SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags): 106, 356 and 724 in 1398 (all Whinnies on their plates, movie 67); 342 to 344 in 1371 (waves 6 to 8); 127, 128 to 135 and 136 in 1023 (the Meerca chase start, waypoints and catch); 158 in 1163; 197 in 842; 200 in 800 (Ixi Raider Chieftain defeated); 233 in 1327 (the end of the rapid-fire step of target practice; 234 marks the target destroyed); 366 in 1300; 708 to 719 in 1381. The exceptions: 341 has no script setter and no script reads it; the journal reads it, and the stage table sets it (stage 7) for some start indices 1 to 8, so in a game begun at start index 0 it stays off (journal only, like 352 and 268, which are also stage-table flags); 182 has no live script setter, because its only script setter is in a routine nothing calls, so in a game begun at start index 0 the lunge stage flag 25 is never set and the squire chain skips the lunge lesson (High; SPEC-0044, 1056; SPEC-0049 for the start indices that set 182); 167 has no fixed-number setter but is computed as 162 + n by the 1171 clog counter.
3. Resolved: the doors of pack 722 (Bogshot Village) enter the interiors 745, 748, 749, 750 and 752, so the apothecary (745) and the bridge builder's house (752) are reached (steps 32 and 34). Pack 722 itself is reached through cells (Medium).
4. Resolved: the statue puzzle in pack 1353 (the Bazaar district) sets flag 694 and opens a load into pack 1392; the cells 1392 -> 1395 -> 1393 -> 1396 -> 1394 lead to the Rogue shrine (Medium for the cell walk). Pack 1394 has a script exit back to pack 1353 (world 158), opened by its trigger 11 (it sets 104 and 353; flag 0 is not needed), so the shrine is not a dead end (High).
5. Resolved: the door from 1363 into the Petpet shrine 1398 opens when four Mortogs are on their targets in 1363 (flag 692 is set then and the door opens in the same visit; on later visits it opens at entry from 692). Flag 355 is only the second condition.
6. Resolved: flags 342, 343 and 344 are set by the 1371 wave controller when waves 6, 7 and 8 are cleared. Pack 1371 is a five-round arena entered from 1350 (SPEC-0041, SPEC-0042). Flag 341 is journal-only (question 2).
7. Resolved (High): 777 is reached by a script load from 758 and 1017 by a script load from 991. Pack 1077 is an unused copy of pack 1129, whose module it shares; nothing loads it, and it stays one of SPEC-0006's 17 unreached packs. Step 41 (the hand-over and flag 305) runs in 1139 and then 1129.
8. Resolved: pack 1023's Meerca chase sets 127 at the start (the package, item 32, is taken; flag 7 also set), 128 to 135 at the waypoints and 136 at the catch (the package is given back, movie 4).
9. Resolved: pack 1300 sets flag 366 when entered with entry message 80, which the finale sends on its return (step 46, Endings), so the epilogue movies 79 and 83 can play.
10. Resolved (High): the Act 1 opening in pack 909 needs 707 on and 1 off. A second block of the same update, with 1 on and 706 off, repeats only the fade, camera and placement, so no single test names flag 1 both on and off (step 2). The act start is applied before movie 88 is shown. The Act 3 start in pack 1327 needs 240 on and 245 off; flags 400, 223 and 397 are each set only while off and are not terms of the gate (step 27).
11. Resolved (High for the script match, Medium-High for the entity order): fight instance 1 (pack 985, flag 281 clear) is Gnarfas; instance 5 (pack 985, flag 281 set) is the Gelert Assassin; instance 12 (pack 1428) is the trap in Queen Fyora's chambers, with Queen Fyora and the Darkest Faerie, ended by script (steps 19, 39 and 44).
12. Resolved (High): trigger firing. The trigger fire step marks a script trigger record as entered: state 1 on the first fire, then 2 while it keeps firing on later ticks; the script step decays a stale state (no fire for more than one tick) to 3 and then back to 0. Every script trigger that starts a main story step fires by the selected hero's own body contact (object 1), with two exceptions: the hit count of 1428 trigger 5 counts hero shots, and 1398 trigger 34 refuses hero fires and is fired by an entity through CollideEntityWithTriggers. No story trigger is unreachable. Pack 1174 trigger 1 (step 21) is a static rectangle fired by the hero's body contact or by a hero shot. Who fires what, where and when: SPEC-0028, Behavior, Trigger pass.
13. Resolved: movie 59 never plays in play (High). Flag 333 is set by pack 1428's act-start sequence before the hero first reaches pack 1411 (world 178), and by the Act 4 start index; no level script clears 333. Pack 1411 trigger 0 plays movie 59 only while 333 is off, so no order of play reaches it. Movie 60 and the mosaic (334) are not affected. Clean behaviour: reproduce the gate as stored; movie 59 stays unplayed.
14. Resolved for script order (High), narrowed for physical access (Medium): in pack 777 (step 30), all five triggers are registered on every load. Index 4 fires on contact, once per load, while 253 is off; it does not test 252, so it can fire before movie 35, and its 252 then silences trigger index 0 (movie 35 and flag 69 skipped). Indices 5 to 8 each need Square and their own flag off; the code sets no order among them, but 256 (index 8) stops the routine and the exit stays shut until 253 to 256 are all on. Static placement data put index 8 on a pedestal similar to the other book pedestals, with ground patches at its top and no library-table object at or around it; the module has no flag gate that requires 253 to 255 first. The lowest ground patch on the pedestal is 14.5 units above the dais top (47 versus 32.5). The documented hero jump peaks about 3.20 units above takeoff, so a direct jump from the dais cannot reach that patch. Pack 777 has three type-10 ladder groups (six records, all ladder-face/top records) and no climb-panel records; the nearest projected horizontal record segment is about 68.43 units from the index-8 trigger center. This rules out a ladder or climb panel beside the middle pedestal, but does not exclude an indirect route through other geometry or interaction from below. Ground-patch and climb records do not establish collision geometry, climbability, or continuous navigation. Therefore no static script or placement support forces the intended book order, while actual first-use reachability remains unconfirmed (SPEC-0033, Behavior 50 and vector 117; SPEC-0006, Type 10; SPEC-0045, pack 777).
15. Rope Gun route requirement: the pack-909 grant is gated by the Free Tor's Farm progress flag and by item absence. In the Glade, the item-36 test selects dialogue only; targets 0–2 are activated in both possession branches. No direct story-completion gate is established. Whether an anchor is physically required to reach a main-route objective remains open.
16. Resolved (R-0148; High for the script and the idle rule, Medium-High for the design reading): Gil the Fence, Drool and Lump in pack 1139 are not fights. They are class-17 rows placed in default mode 0, so they never notice the hero, and the 1139 module only registers talk entries for them. The hero stops at about 3.2 from their centres by body radii (1.175 and 2.0), not at 4.5, and no wall, object or movable block stands near them. Details and the default: Edge cases, thieves' crew in 1139. Still open: why a rewrite build holds the hero at about 4.5 is not explained by disc data.
